187: function isoDate(year, month, day) {
188:   return `${year}-${String(month).padStart(2,"0")}-${String(day).padStart(2,"0")}`;
189: }
190: function splitCalendarBlocks(text) {
191:   return String(text||"").replace(/\r/g,"").split(/\n\s*\n+/).map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
192: }
193: function parseTimeRange(text) {
194:   const m=String(text||"").match(/(?:^|\s)([01]?\d|2[0-3])[:：](\d{2})\s*(?:[-~～]|부터)\s*([01]?\d|2[0-3])[:：](\d{2})/);
195:   if(m) return [`${m[1].padStart(2,"0")}:${m[2]}`,`${m[3].padStart(2,"0")}:${m[4]}`];
196:   const one=String(text||"").match(/(?:^|\s)([01]?\d|2[0-3])[:：](\d{2})/);
197:   return one ? [`${one[1].padStart(2,"0")}:${one[2]}`,""] : ["",""];
198: }
199: function classifyCalendarStatus(text) {
200:   const value=String(text||"");
201:   if(/취소|폐지/.test(value)) return "취소";
202:   if(/확정/.test(value)) return "확정";
203:   if(/예약|대관|사용/.test(value)) return "예약";
204:   if(/예정|계획|공고|제출기간/.test(value)) return "예정";
205:   return "상태 미기재";
206: }
207: function parseOwner(text) {
208:   const m=String(text||"").match(/^\(([^)]+)\)/);
209:   const token=(m?.[1]||"").trim();
210:   if(!token) return ["",""];
211:   if(/부$|부\s/.test(token)) return [token.replace(/\s*확정$/,""),""];
212:   const parts=token.split(/\s+/);
213:   if(parts.length>1 && /부$/.test(parts[0])) return [parts[0],parts.slice(1).join(" ")];
214:   return ["",token.replace(/\s*확정$/," ").trim()];
215: }

219:   return response.status===204?{}:response.json();
220: }

242: function validateServiceAccount(value) {
243:   return Boolean(
244:     value && value.type === "service_account" &&
245:     typeof value.client_email === "string" &&
246:     typeof value.private_key === "string" &&
247:     value.private_key.startsWith("-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----") &&
248:     value.token_uri === "https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token"
249:   );
250: }
251: 
252: function recoveryKey(salt) {
253:   // safeStorage가 Windows/Chromium 키 문제로 복호화에 실패할 때만 사용하는 로컬 복구키입니다.
254:   // 동일 Windows 사용자/PC에서 재부팅 후에도 안정적으로 재생성됩니다.
255:   const identity = `${os.hostname()}|${os.userInfo().username}|UEP-CREDENTIAL-RECOVERY-v1`;
256:   return crypto.scryptSync(identity, salt, 32);
257: }
258: 
259: async function writeCredentialRecovery(value) {
260:   const salt = crypto.randomBytes(16), iv = crypto.randomBytes(12);
261:   const key = recoveryKey(salt);
262:   const cipher = crypto.createCipheriv("aes-256-gcm", key, iv);
263:   const encrypted = Buffer.concat([cipher.update(JSON.stringify(value), "utf8"), cipher.final()]);
264:   const payload = {
265:     version: 1, algorithm: "aes-256-gcm",
266:     salt: salt.toString("base64"), iv: iv.toString("base64"),
267:     tag: cipher.getAuthTag().toString("base64"), data: encrypted.toString("base64")
268:   };
269:   await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(credentialRecoveryPath()), { recursive: true });
270:   await fs.writeFile(credentialRecoveryPath(), JSON.stringify(payload), "utf8");
271: }
272: 
273: async function readCredentialRecovery() {
274:   const payload = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(credentialRecoveryPath(), "utf8"));
275:   if (!payload || payload.version !== 1 || payload.algorithm !== "aes-256-gcm") throw new Error("invalid recovery credential");
276:   const salt=Buffer.from(payload.salt,"base64"), iv=Buffer.from(payload.iv,"base64"), tag=Buffer.from(payload.tag,"base64"), data=Buffer.from(payload.data,"base64");
277:   const decipher = crypto.createDecipheriv("aes-256-gcm", recoveryKey(salt), iv);
278:   decipher.setAuthTag(tag);
279:   return JSON.parse(Buffer.concat([decipher.update(data), decipher.final()]).toString("utf8"));
280: }
281: 
282: async function saveEncrypted(filePath, value) {
283:   await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(filePath), { recursive: true });
284:   if (safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()) {
285:     await fs.writeFile(filePath, safeStorage.encryptString(JSON.stringify(value)));
286:   } else {
287:     // 인증정보는 아래 복구 저장소가 있으므로, 다른 보안값은 기존처럼 실패시킵니다.
288:     if (path.resolve(filePath) !== path.resolve(credentialPath())) throw new Error("Windows 보안 저장소를 사용할 수 없습니다.");
289:   }
290:   if (path.resolve(filePath) === path.resolve(credentialPath())) await writeCredentialRecovery(value);
291: }

293: // __UEP_GOOGLE_MISSING_CREDENTIAL_08069__
294: async function readEncrypted(filePath) {
295:   let encrypted = null;
296:   try { encrypted = await fs.readFile(filePath); } catch (readError) {
297:     if (path.resolve(filePath) === path.resolve(credentialPath())) {
298:       try { return await readCredentialRecovery(); } catch {}
299:     }
300:     if (path.resolve(filePath) === path.resolve(credentialPath()) && (readError?.code === "ENOENT" || /no such file/i.test(String(readError?.message||"")))) {
301:       const friendly = new Error("담임 배포 PC의 Google 시트 연결 승인이 아직 준비되지 않았습니다.");
302:       friendly.code = "UEP_GOOGLE_CREDENTIAL_MISSING";
303:       friendly.setupRequired = true;
304:       throw friendly;
305:     }
306:     throw readError;
307:   }
308:   if (safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()) {
309:     try { return JSON.parse(safeStorage.decryptString(encrypted)); } catch {}
310:   }
311:   // 일부 구버전/수동 이관 파일이 평문 JSON인 경우 현재 형식으로 재저장합니다.
312:   try {
313:     const legacy = JSON.parse(encrypted.toString("utf8"));
314:     if (legacy && typeof legacy === "object") { await saveEncrypted(filePath, legacy); return legacy; }
315:   } catch {}
316:   // Google 서비스 계정은 safeStorage가 깨져도 동일 PC/사용자용 AES-GCM 복구본으로 자동 복원합니다.
317:   if (path.resolve(filePath) === path.resolve(credentialPath())) {
318:     try {
319:       const recovered = await readCredentialRecovery();
320:       if (validateServiceAccount(recovered)) {
321:         try { if (safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()) await fs.writeFile(filePath, safeStorage.encryptString(JSON.stringify(recovered))); } catch {}
322:         return recovered;
323:       }
324:     } catch {}
325:   }
326:   const backup = `${filePath}.unreadable-${Date.now()}.bak`;
327:   try { await fs.rename(filePath, backup); } catch {}
328:   const error = new Error("저장된 보안 인증정보를 복호화할 수 없습니다. 서비스 계정 JSON을 다시 등록해 주세요.");
329:   error.code = "UEP_SAFE_STORAGE_DECRYPT_FAILED";
330:   throw error;
331: }

398: async function loadGoogleOAuthClientId(){
399:   if(googleOAuthPolicyCache.clientId && googleOAuthPolicyCache.expiresAt>Date.now()) return googleOAuthPolicyCache.clientId;
400:   let response;
401:   try{ response=await net.fetch(UEP_UPDATE_POLICY_URL,{cache:"no-store"}); }
402:   catch(error){ throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 연결 설정을 확인하지 못했습니다. 잠시 후 다시 시도해 주세요.","UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_POLICY_UNAVAILABLE"); }

410: function googleUserOAuthRecoveryKey(salt){
411:   const identity=`${os.hostname()}|${os.userInfo().username}|UEP-GOOGLE-USER-OAUTH-v1`;
412:   return crypto.scryptSync(identity,salt,32);
413: }
414: function encodeGoogleUserOAuthRecovery(value){
415:   const salt=crypto.randomBytes(16),iv=crypto.randomBytes(12),key=googleUserOAuthRecoveryKey(salt);
416:   const cipher=crypto.createCipheriv('aes-256-gcm',key,iv);
417:   const encrypted=Buffer.concat([cipher.update(JSON.stringify(value),'utf8'),cipher.final()]);
418:   return {version:1,algorithm:'aes-256-gcm',salt:salt.toString('base64'),iv:iv.toString('base64'),tag:cipher.getAuthTag().toString('base64'),data:encrypted.toString('base64')};
419: }
420: function decodeGoogleUserOAuthRecovery(payload){
421:   if(!payload||payload.version!==1||payload.algorithm!=='aes-256-gcm')throw new Error('invalid OAuth recovery token');
422:   const salt=Buffer.from(payload.salt,'base64'),iv=Buffer.from(payload.iv,'base64'),tag=Buffer.from(payload.tag,'base64'),data=Buffer.from(payload.data,'base64');
423:   const decipher=crypto.createDecipheriv('aes-256-gcm',googleUserOAuthRecoveryKey(salt),iv);decipher.setAuthTag(tag);
424:   return JSON.parse(Buffer.concat([decipher.update(data),decipher.final()]).toString('utf8'));
425: }
426: async function writeGoogleUserOAuthRecovery(value){
427:   const payload=encodeGoogleUserOAuthRecovery(value);
428:   for(const target of [googleUserOAuthRecoveryPath(),googleUserOAuthCanonicalRecoveryPath()]){
429:     await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(target),{recursive:true});
430:     await fs.writeFile(target,JSON.stringify(payload),'utf8');
431:   }
432: }
433: async function readGoogleUserOAuthRecovery(){
434:   for(const target of [googleUserOAuthCanonicalRecoveryPath(),googleUserOAuthRecoveryPath()]){
435:     try{return decodeGoogleUserOAuthRecovery(JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(target,'utf8')));}catch{}
436:   }
437:   throw new Error('OAuth recovery token not found');
438: }
439: async function saveGoogleUserOAuth(value){
440:   let primarySaved=false;
441:   if(safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()){
442:     const encrypted=safeStorage.encryptString(JSON.stringify(value));
443:     for(const target of [googleUserOAuthCanonicalPath(),googleUserOAuthPath()]){
444:       try{await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(target),{recursive:true});await fs.writeFile(target,encrypted);primarySaved=true;}catch{}
445:     }
446:   }
447:   await writeGoogleUserOAuthRecovery(value);
448:   return {primarySaved,recoverySaved:true,canonical:true};
449: }
450: async function readGoogleUserOAuth(){
451:   for(const target of [googleUserOAuthCanonicalPath(),googleUserOAuthPath()]){
452:     try{
453:       const encrypted=await fs.readFile(target);
454:       if(safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()){
455:         try{const value=JSON.parse(safeStorage.decryptString(encrypted));if(value&&typeof value==='object'){await saveGoogleUserOAuth(value);return value;}}catch{}
456:       }
457:       try{const legacy=JSON.parse(encrypted.toString('utf8'));if(legacy&&typeof legacy==='object'){await saveGoogleUserOAuth(legacy);return legacy;}}catch{}
458:     }catch{}
459:   }
460:   try{
461:     const recovered=await readGoogleUserOAuthRecovery();
462:     if(recovered&&typeof recovered==='object'){await saveGoogleUserOAuth(recovered);return recovered;}
463:   }catch{}
464:   return null;
465: }
466: async function googleUserInfo(accessToken){

471: async function refreshGoogleUserOAuth(saved){
472:   if(!saved?.refresh_token) throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 연결을 다시 승인해 주세요.","UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
473:   const clientId=await loadGoogleOAuthClientId();
474:   if(String(saved.client_id||'').trim() && String(saved.client_id).trim()!==clientId){
475:     await disconnectGoogleUser();
476:     throw googleOAuthError('Google 연결 설정이 갱신되었습니다. Google 계정 연결을 한 번만 다시 승인해 주세요.','UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED');
477:   }
478:   const body=new URLSearchParams({client_id:clientId,refresh_token:String(saved.refresh_token),grant_type:'refresh_token'});
479:   const response=await net.fetch('https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token',{method:'POST',headers:{'content-type':'application/x-www-form-urlencoded'},body:body.toString()});
480:   if(!response.ok){
481:     let payload={},detail='',errCode='';
482:     try{payload=await response.json();detail=String(payload?.error_description||'');errCode=String(payload?.error||'');}catch{}
483:     if(errCode==='invalid_client'||errCode==='invalid_grant'){
484:       googleOAuthPolicyCache={clientId:'',expiresAt:0};
485:       await disconnectGoogleUser();
486:       throw googleOAuthError('저장된 Google 인증정보가 현재 UEP 연결설정과 맞지 않습니다. Google 계정 연결을 다시 승인해 주세요.','UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED');
487:     }
488:     throw googleOAuthError(`Google 계정 토큰 갱신 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:''}`,"UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
489:   }
490:   const next=await response.json();
491:   const merged={...saved,...next,client_id:clientId,expires_at:Date.now()+Math.max(60,Number(next.expires_in||3600))*1000,updated_at:new Date().toISOString()};
492:   await saveGoogleUserOAuth(merged);
493:   return merged;
494: }

501:   return String(saved.access_token);

509: async function authorizeGoogleUser({loginHint=''}={}){
510:   const clientId=await loadGoogleOAuthClientId();
511:   const verifier=crypto.randomBytes(48).toString('base64url');
512:   const challenge=crypto.createHash('sha256').update(verifier).digest('base64url');
513:   const state=crypto.randomBytes(24).toString('base64url');
514:   let timer=null;
515:   let settled=false;
516:   let callbackResolve,callbackReject;
517:   const callbackPromise=new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{callbackResolve=resolve;callbackReject=reject;});
518:   const server=http.createServer((req,res)=>{
519:     try{
520:       const u=new URL(req.url||'/', 'http://127.0.0.1');
521:       if(u.pathname!=='/' && u.pathname!=='/oauth2callback'){res.writeHead(404);res.end('Not found');return;}
522:       const returnedState=String(u.searchParams.get('state')||'');
523:       const code=String(u.searchParams.get('code')||'');
524:       const error=String(u.searchParams.get('error')||'');
525:       res.writeHead(200,{'content-type':'text/html; charset=utf-8','cache-control':'no-store'});
526:       if(error||returnedState!==state||!code){
527:         res.end('<!doctype html><meta charset="utf-8"><title>UEP Google 연결</title><h2>UEP Google 계정 연결이 취소되었거나 실패했습니다.</h2><p>이 창을 닫고 UEP에서 다시 시도해 주세요.</p>');
528:         if(!settled){settled=true;callbackReject(googleOAuthError(error?`Google 승인 취소: ${error}`:'Google 승인 응답 검증에 실패했습니다.','UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_CALLBACK_FAILED'));}
529:         return;
530:       }
531:       res.end('<!doctype html><meta charset="utf-8"><title>UEP Google 연결</title><h2>Google 승인 정보가 UEP로 전달되었습니다.</h2><p>이 창을 닫고 UEP로 돌아가 연결 완료 여부를 확인해 주세요.</p>');
532:       if(!settled){settled=true;callbackResolve(code);}
533:     }catch(error){if(!settled){settled=true;callbackReject(error);}}
534:   });
535:   try{
536:     await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{server.once('error',reject);server.listen(0,'127.0.0.1',resolve);});
537:     const address=server.address();
538:     const port=typeof address==='object'&&address?address.port:0;
539:     if(!port) throw googleOAuthError('Google 승인용 로컬 포트를 열지 못했습니다.','UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_CALLBACK_FAILED');
540:     const redirectUri=`http://127.0.0.1:${port}`;
541:     const authUrl=new URL('https://accounts.google.com/o/oauth2/v2/auth');
542:     authUrl.searchParams.set('client_id',clientId);
543:     authUrl.searchParams.set('redirect_uri',redirectUri);
544:     authUrl.searchParams.set('response_type','code');
545:     authUrl.searchParams.set('scope','openid email https://www.googleapis.com/auth/spreadsheets.readonly');
546:     authUrl.searchParams.set('code_challenge',challenge);
547:     authUrl.searchParams.set('code_challenge_method','S256');
548:     authUrl.searchParams.set('state',state);
549:     authUrl.searchParams.set('access_type','offline');
550:     authUrl.searchParams.set('prompt','consent');
551:     if(String(loginHint||'').includes('@')) authUrl.searchParams.set('login_hint',String(loginHint).trim());
552:     await shell.openExternal(authUrl.toString());
553:     timer=setTimeout(()=>{if(!settled){settled=true;callbackReject(googleOAuthError('Google 계정 승인이 시간 초과되었습니다.','UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_TIMEOUT'));}},180000);
554:     const code=await callbackPromise;
555:     const body=new URLSearchParams();
556:     body.set('client_id',clientId);
557: 
558:     body.set('code',code);
559:     body.set('code_verifier',verifier);
560:     body.set('redirect_uri',redirectUri);
561:     body.set('grant_type','authorization_code');
562:     const response=await net.fetch('https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token',{method:'POST',headers:{'content-type':'application/x-www-form-urlencoded;charset=UTF-8','accept':'application/json'},body:body.toString()});
563:     if(!response.ok){
564:       let errCode='',detail='',raw='';
565:       try{const payload=await response.json();errCode=String(payload?.error||'');detail=String(payload?.error_description||'');raw=JSON.stringify(payload);}catch{try{raw=await response.text();}catch{}}
566:       const suffix=[errCode,detail].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
567:       if(errCode==='invalid_client'){
568:         googleOAuthPolicyCache={clientId:'',expiresAt:0};
569:         await disconnectGoogleUser();
570:         throw googleOAuthError('Google OAuth 토큰 교환이 거부되었습니다. UEP의 Desktop OAuth Client ID와 Google 승인 설정을 확인한 뒤 다시 연결해 주세요.','UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED');
571:       }
572:       throw googleOAuthError(`Google 토큰 교환 실패 (${response.status})${suffix?`: ${suffix}`:''}${!suffix&&raw?`: ${raw}`:''}`,'UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_TOKEN_FAILED');
573:     }
574:     const tokenPayload=await response.json();
575:     if(!tokenPayload.access_token) throw googleOAuthError('Google 액세스 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.','UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_TOKEN_FAILED');
576:     let info={};
577:     try{info=await googleUserInfo(tokenPayload.access_token);}catch{}
578:     let idTokenInfo={};
579:     try{
580:       const parts=String(tokenPayload.id_token||'').split('.');
581:       if(parts.length>=2) idTokenInfo=JSON.parse(Buffer.from(parts[1].replace(/-/g,'+').replace(/_/g,'/'),'base64').toString('utf8'))||{};
582:     }catch{}
583:     const fallbackEmail=String(loginHint||'').trim();
584:     const saved={...tokenPayload,client_id:clientId,email:String(info?.email||idTokenInfo?.email||fallbackEmail||''),sub:String(info?.sub||idTokenInfo?.sub||''),expires_at:Date.now()+Math.max(60,Number(tokenPayload.expires_in||3600))*1000,created_at:new Date().toISOString(),updated_at:new Date().toISOString()};
585:     const persistResult=await saveGoogleUserOAuth(saved);
586:     const verifySaved=await readGoogleUserOAuth();
587:     if(!verifySaved?.access_token) throw googleOAuthError('Google 승인 토큰 저장 확인에 실패했습니다.','UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_PERSIST_FAILED');
588:     liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;
589:     let syncOk=false, syncError='', syncCode='', data=null;
590:     try {
591:       data=await fetchLiveData({force:true});
592:       syncOk=true;
593:     } catch(error) {
594:       syncError=String(error?.message||error||'Google 시트 조회 실패');
595:       syncCode=String(error?.code||'');
596:     }
597:     return {ok:true,authorized:true,mode:'user_oauth',account:saved.email,sourceName:data?.sourceName||UEP_SPREADSHEET_NAME,preview:data?.summary||null,syncedAt:data?.syncedAt||'',syncOk,syncError,syncCode};
598:   }finally{
599:     if(timer)clearTimeout(timer);
600:     try{server.close();}catch{}
601:   }
602: }
603: async function disconnectGoogleUser(){
604:   for(const file of [googleUserOAuthCanonicalPath(),googleUserOAuthCanonicalRecoveryPath(),googleUserOAuthPath(),googleUserOAuthRecoveryPath()]){
605:     try{await fs.unlink(file);}catch(error){if(error?.code!=='ENOENT')throw error;}
606:   }
607:   liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;return {ok:true};
608: }
609: async function getSheetsToken(credentials) {
610:   const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
611:   const header = base64url(JSON.stringify({ alg: "RS256", typ: "JWT" }));
612:   const claims = base64url(JSON.stringify({
613:     iss: credentials.client_email,
614:     scope: "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/spreadsheets",
615:     aud: credentials.token_uri,
616:     iat: now,
617:     exp: now + 3600,
618:   }));
619:   const unsigned = `${header}.${claims}`;
620:   const signature = crypto.sign("RSA-SHA256", Buffer.from(unsigned), credentials.private_key).toString("base64url");
621:   const body = new URLSearchParams({
622:     grant_type: "urn:ietf:params:oauth:grant-type:jwt-bearer",
623:     assertion: `${unsigned}.${signature}`,
624:   });
625:   const response = await fetch(credentials.token_uri, {
626:     method: "POST",
627:     headers: { "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
628:     body,
629:   });
630:   if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Google 인증 실패 (${response.status})`);
631:   const payload = await response.json();
632:   if (!payload.access_token) throw new Error("Google 인증 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.");
633:   return payload.access_token;
634: }

636: async function readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt = 0) {
637:   if(String(token||'').startsWith('UEP_SCHOOL_READ:')) return schoolReadBatchRead(spreadsheetId,ranges);
638:   const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchGet`);
639:   url.searchParams.set("majorDimension", "ROWS");
640:   url.searchParams.set("valueRenderOption", "UNFORMATTED_VALUE");
641:   url.searchParams.set("dateTimeRenderOption", "SERIAL_NUMBER");
642:   ranges.forEach((range) => url.searchParams.append("ranges", range));
643:   const response = await fetch(url, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
644:   if (!response.ok) {
645:     let detail = "";
646:     try { detail = (await response.json())?.error?.message || ""; } catch {}
647:     if ((response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) && attempt < 3) {
648:       const retryAfter = Number(response.headers.get("retry-after") || 0);
649:       const waitMs = retryAfter > 0 ? retryAfter * 1000 : 900 * Math.pow(2, attempt);
650:       await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, waitMs));
651:       return readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt + 1);
652:     }
653:     throw new Error(`UEP 구글시트 읽기 실패 (${response.status})${detail ? `: ${detail}` : ""}`);
654:   }
655:   return (await response.json()).valueRanges || [];
656: }
657: 
658: function sheetNameFromA1Range(range="") {
659:   const prefix=String(range||"").split("!")[0].trim();
660:   return prefix.replace(/^'/,"").replace(/'$/,"").replace(/''/g,"'");
661: }
662: 
663: function assignValueRangesByRange(target, entries, valueRanges) {
664:   const logicalBySheet=new Map(entries.map(([logicalName,range])=>[sheetNameFromA1Range(range),logicalName]));
665:   const assigned=new Set();
666:   (Array.isArray(valueRanges)?valueRanges:[]).forEach((item,index)=>{
667:     const responseSheet=sheetNameFromA1Range(item?.range);
668:     // Sheets batchGet may omit empty ranges or return them in a different order.
669:     // A response that names its range must only be assigned to that sheet; index is
670:     // retained solely for legacy responses that contain no range metadata at all.
671:     const logicalName=responseSheet?logicalBySheet.get(responseSheet):entries[index]?.[0];
672:     if(!logicalName)return;
673:     target[logicalName]=item?.values||[];
674:     assigned.add(logicalName);
675:   });
676:   entries.forEach(([logicalName])=>{if(!assigned.has(logicalName)&&!target[logicalName])target[logicalName]=[];});
677:   return target;
678: }
679: 
680: async function updateSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, range, values, attempt=0) {
681:   const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(range)}`);
682:   url.searchParams.set("valueInputOption", "USER_ENTERED");
683:   const response = await fetch(url, {
684:     method: "PUT",
685:     headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
686:     body: JSON.stringify({ majorDimension: "ROWS", values }),
687:   });
688:   if (!response.ok) {
689:     let detail=""; try{ detail=(await response.json())?.error?.message||""; }catch{}
690:     if ((response.status===429 || response.status>=500) && attempt<4) {
691:       const retryAfter=Number(response.headers.get("retry-after")||0);
692:       const waitMs=retryAfter>0?retryAfter*1000:1000*Math.pow(2,attempt);
693:       await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,waitMs));
694:       return updateSheetValues(token,spreadsheetId,range,values,attempt+1);
695:     }
696:     throw new Error(`구글시트 저장 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:""}`);
697:   }
698:   return response.json();
699: }
700: 
701: async function batchUpdateSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, data, attempt=0) {
702:   const items=(Array.isArray(data)?data:[]).filter(x=>x&&x.range&&Array.isArray(x.values));
703:   if(!items.length)return {totalUpdatedRows:0};
704:   const url=new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchUpdate`);
705:   const response=await fetch(url,{
706:     method:"POST",
707:     headers:{Authorization:`Bearer ${token}`,"Content-Type":"application/json"},
708:     body:JSON.stringify({valueInputOption:"USER_ENTERED",data:items.map(x=>({range:x.range,majorDimension:"ROWS",values:x.values}))}),
709:   });
710:   if(!response.ok){
711:     let detail="";try{detail=(await response.json())?.error?.message||"";}catch{}
712:     if((response.status===429||response.status>=500)&&attempt<4){
713:       const retryAfter=Number(response.headers.get("retry-after")||0);
714:       const waitMs=retryAfter>0?retryAfter*1000:1000*Math.pow(2,attempt);
715:       await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,waitMs));
716:       return batchUpdateSheetValues(token,spreadsheetId,items,attempt+1);
717:     }
718:     throw new Error(`구글시트 일괄 저장 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:""}`);
719:   }
720:   return response.json();
721: }

724: function columnLetter(index) {
725:   let n=Number(index)+1, out="";
726:   while(n>0){ const r=(n-1)%26; out=String.fromCharCode(65+r)+out; n=Math.floor((n-1)/26); }
727:   return out;
728: }
729: function normalizeSheetHeader(value){return String(value??"").replace(/\r?\n/g," ").replace(/\s+/g," ").trim();}
730: function findHeaderRowByRequired(matrix,required){
731:   const wanted=required.map(x=>String(x));
732:   const limit=Math.min(Array.isArray(matrix)?matrix.length:0,12);
733:   for(let i=0;i<limit;i+=1){
734:     const row=(matrix[i]||[]).map(normalizeSheetHeader);
735:     if(wanted.every(name=>row.includes(name))) return {index:i,headers:row};
736:   }
737:   return null;
738: }
739: async function saveStudentContact(payload={}) {
740:   const allowed={phone:["학생연락처"],email:["학생이메일","이메일","이메일주소"],guardian1Phone:["보호자1연락처"],guardian2Phone:["보호자2연락처"]};
741:   const field=String(payload.field||"");
742:   if(!allowed[field]) throw new Error("수정할 수 없는 학생정보 항목입니다.");
743:   const role=String(payload.role||"");
744:   if(!["admin","grade_head","homeroom"].includes(role)) throw new Error("학생 연락정보 수정 권한이 없습니다."); /* UEP_08283_CONTACT_WRITE_SCOPE */
745:   const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
746:   if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("저장된 Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 올바르지 않습니다.");
747:   const token=await getSheetsToken(account);
748:   const range="'04_학생연락식별정보'!A1:Z1000";
749:   const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,[range]);
750:   const matrix=result?.[0]?.values||[];
751:   const header=findHeaderRowByRequired(matrix,["학생ID"]);
752:   if(!header) throw new Error("04_학생연락식별정보의 헤더에서 학생ID를 찾지 못했습니다.");
753:   const studentId=String(payload.studentId||"").trim();
754:   if(!studentId) throw new Error("학생ID가 없어 저장할 수 없습니다.");
755:   const idCol=header.headers.indexOf("학생ID");
756:   let fieldCol=-1;
757:   for(const name of allowed[field]){ const idx=header.headers.indexOf(name); if(idx>=0){fieldCol=idx;break;} }
758:   if(fieldCol<0) throw new Error(`04_학생연락식별정보에 ${allowed[field][0]} 열이 없습니다.`);
759:   let dataRow=-1;
760:   for(let r=header.index+1;r<matrix.length;r+=1){ if(String(matrix[r]?.[idCol]||"").trim()===studentId){dataRow=r;break;} }
761:   const value=String(payload.value??"").trim();
762:   if(dataRow<0){
763:     const row=Array(Math.max(header.headers.length,fieldCol+1)).fill("");
764:     row[idCol]=studentId; row[fieldCol]=value;
765:     const useCol=header.headers.indexOf("현재사용여부"); if(useCol>=0)row[useCol]="Y";
766:     await appendSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,"'04_학생연락식별정보'!A:Z",[row]);
767:   }else{
768:     const sheetRow=dataRow+1;
769:     const col=columnLetter(fieldCol);
770:     await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'04_학생연락식별정보'!${col}${sheetRow}:${col}${sheetRow}`,[[value]]);
771:   }
772:   if(liveDataCache?.students){liveDataCache={...liveDataCache,students:liveDataCache.students.map(student=>String(student.id||'')===studentId?{...student,[field]:value}:student)};liveDataFetchedAt=Date.now();}
773:   return {ok:true,contactPatch:{studentId,field,value}};
774: }

906: async function clearSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, range) {
907:   const url = `https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(range)}:clear`;
908:   const response = await fetch(url, {
909:     method: "POST",
910:     headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
911:     body: "{}",
912:   });
913:   if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`공결 삭제 실패 (${response.status})`);
914:   return response.json();

917: function officialAttendanceRow(payload={}, id="") {
918:   const now=new Date().toISOString();
919:   const type=String(payload.detailType||"결석");
920:   const start=String(payload.startPeriod||"");
921:   const end=String(payload.endPeriod||"");
922:   const unit=(start||end)?"교시":"일일";
923:   const scope=(start||end)?(start&&end?`${start}~${end}교시`:`${start||end}교시`):"하루 전체";
924:   return [[
925:     id,
926:     String(payload.studentId||""), String(payload.studentNo||""), String(payload.name||""),
927:     String(payload.grade||"1"), String(payload.className||payload.classNo||""), String(payload.date||""),
928:     String(payload.attendanceType||"출석인정"), type, start, end,

937: async function saveOfficialAttendance(payload={}) {
938:   const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
939:   if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("저장된 Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 올바르지 않습니다.");
940:   const token=await getSheetsToken(account);
941:   const range="'30_공결기록'!A3:Z3000";
942:   const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,[range]);
943:   const matrix=result?.[0]?.values||[];
944:   const rows=matrix.slice(1);
945:   let id=String(payload.id||"").trim();
946:   let sheetRow=-1;
947:   if(id){
948:     const pos=rows.findIndex(row=>String(row?.[0]||"").trim()===id);
949:     if(pos>=0) sheetRow=pos+4;
950:   }else{
951:     let max=0;

954:   }

956:   if(sheetRow>0) await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'30_공결기록'!A${sheetRow}:Z${sheetRow}`,values);
957:   else await appendSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,"'30_공결기록'!A:Z",values);
958:   const operational=await fetchOperationalData();
959:   return {ok:true,id,patch:operational.patch||{}};
960: }

1004:   return [[
1005:     id,

1007:     String(payload.name||""),
1008:     String(payload.start||""),

1014:     String(payload.lateEntry||""),
1015:     String(payload.earlyExit||""),
1016:     String(payload.reason||""),

1018:     payload.active===false?"N":"Y",
1019:     String(payload.note||""),
1020:     nowIso,
1021:     String(payload.writer||"UEP"),
1022:     String(payload.source||"UEP 규칙센터"),

1031: function dormRuleDateValue(value){
1032:   if(value===null||value===undefined||value==='')return '';
1033:   const raw=String(value).trim();
1034:   if(/^\d+(?:\.\d+)?$/.test(raw)){
1035:     const serial=Number(raw);
1036:     if(Number.isFinite(serial)&&serial>20000&&serial<100000){
1037:       const d=new Date(Date.UTC(1899,11,30)+Math.round(serial*86400000));
1038:       if(!Number.isNaN(d.getTime())){
1039:         const y=d.getUTCFullYear(),mo=String(d.getUTCMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0'),day=String(d.getUTCDate()).padStart(2,'0');
1040:         const hh=String(d.getUTCHours()).padStart(2,'0'),mm=String(d.getUTCMinutes()).padStart(2,'0');
1041:         return `${y}-${mo}-${day}T${hh}:${mm}`;
1042:       }
1043:     }
1044:   }
1045:   const mm=raw.replace(' ','T').match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{1,2})-(\d{1,2})T(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
1046:   return mm?`${mm[1]}-${String(mm[2]).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(mm[3]).padStart(2,'0')}T${String(mm[4]).padStart(2,'0')}:${mm[5]}`:raw;
1047: }
1048: function dormRuleObjectFromRow(row=[]){
1049:   return {
1050:     id:String(row[0]||'').trim(),category:String(row[1]||'').trim(),name:String(row[2]||'').trim(),
1051:     start:dormRuleDateValue(row[3]),end:dormRuleDateValue(row[4]),days:String(row[5]||'전체').trim(),
1052:     effect:String(row[6]||'').trim(),entryTime:String(row[7]||'').trim(),exitTime:String(row[8]||'').trim(),
1053:     lateEntry:String(row[9]||'').trim(),earlyExit:String(row[10]||'').trim(),reason:String(row[11]||'').trim(),
1054:     priority:Number(row[12]||dormRulePriority(row[1])),active:String(row[13]||'Y').trim().toUpperCase()!=='N',
1055:     note:String(row[14]||'').trim(),modifiedAt:String(row[15]||'').trim(),writer:String(row[16]||'').trim(),source:String(row[17]||'').trim(),
1056:     operationState:String(row[18]||'').trim(),actualExit:dormRuleDateValue(row[19]),actualReentry:dormRuleDateValue(row[20]),
1057:     priorNightAcademyHome:/^(Y|YES|TRUE|1)$/i.test(String(row[21]||'')),
1058:     formRequired:/^(Y|YES|TRUE|1)$/i.test(String(row[22]||'')),
1059:     individualOutingAllowed:/^(Y|YES|TRUE|1)$/i.test(String(row[23]||''))
1060:   };
1061: }

1132: async function saveDormOuting(payload={}){
1133:   const allowedRoles=new Set(['admin','grade_head','grade_manager','homeroom']);
1134:   if(payload.role&&!allowedRoles.has(String(payload.role)))throw new Error('학사외출 교사등록 권한이 없습니다.');

1136:   if(!date||!studentNo||!name)throw new Error('학생·학번·날짜 정보를 확인해 주세요.');
1137:   const home=/퇴소|귀가/.test(category),outTime=String(payload.outTime||''),returnTime=String(payload.returnTime||'');
1138:   if(!outTime)throw new Error('외출·귀가 시간을 입력해 주세요.');

1143:   const dcol=col('외출일자','신청일자','운영일','일자','날짜'),ncol=col('학번','학생학번'),idcol=col('학생ID','학생 ID'),ccol=col('외출구분','신청구분','구분'),tcol=col('외출시간','출발예정시간','출발시간');
1144:   const duplicate=matrix.slice(1).some(r=>String(r[dcol]||'').slice(0,10)===date&&((ncol>=0&&String(r[ncol]||'').replace(/\.0$/,'')===studentNo)||(idcol>=0&&String(r[idcol]||'')===String(payload.studentId||'')))&&String(r[ccol]||'')===category&&String(r[tcol]||'')===outTime);

1309: async function resolveInputAsset(fileValue=''){const raw=String(fileValue||'').trim();if(!raw)return '';const direct=path.isAbsolute(raw)?raw:path.join(stableUserDataRoot(),raw);try{await fs.access(direct);return direct;}catch{return '';}}
1310: async function readInputCenterAsset(fileValue=''){const filePath=await resolveInputAsset(fileValue);if(!filePath)return {ok:false,reason:'원본 파일을 찾지 못했습니다.'};const ext=path.extname(filePath).toLowerCase();const mime={'.jpg':'image/jpeg','.jpeg':'image/jpeg','.png':'image/png','.webp':'image/webp','.gif':'image/gif'}[ext];if(!mime)return {ok:false,reason:'화면 미리보기는 JPG·PNG·WEBP 이미지를 지원합니다.',path:filePath};const data=await fs.readFile(filePath);return {ok:true,path:filePath,name:path.basename(filePath),dataUrl:`${'data:'}${mime};base64,${data.toString('base64')}`};}
1311: async function openInputCenterAsset(fileValue=''){const filePath=await resolveInputAsset(fileValue);if(!filePath)return {ok:false,reason:'원본 파일을 찾지 못했습니다.'};const reason=await shell.openPath(filePath);return {ok:!reason,reason,path:filePath};}

1313: async function runProcess(command,args=[],options={}){
1314:   return new Promise((resolve)=>{
1315:     let settled=false;const finish=(v)=>{if(!settled){settled=true;resolve(v)}};
1316:     try{const child=spawn(command,args,{windowsHide:true,...options});let stdout='',stderr='';
1317:       child.stdout?.on('data',d=>stdout+=d.toString());child.stderr?.on('data',d=>stderr+=d.toString());
1318:       child.on('error',e=>finish({ok:false,code:-1,stdout,stderr:String(e?.message||e)}));
1319:       child.on('close',code=>finish({ok:code===0,code,stdout,stderr}));
1320:     }catch(e){finish({ok:false,code:-1,stdout:'',stderr:String(e?.message||e)})}
1321:   });
1322: }
1323: async function ocrWithTesseract(filePath){
1324:   const probes=process.platform==='win32'?[path.join(process.env.ProgramFiles||'C:/Program Files','Tesseract-OCR','tesseract.exe'),'tesseract.exe']:['tesseract'];
1325:   for(const exe of probes){const r=await runProcess(exe,[filePath,'stdout','-l','kor+eng','--psm','6']);if(r.ok&&r.stdout.trim())return {ok:true,engine:'Tesseract',text:r.stdout.trim(),detail:'kor+eng · PSM 6'};}
1326:   return {ok:false,reason:'Tesseract OCR 엔진을 찾지 못했습니다.'};
1327: }
1328: async function ocrWithWindowsMedia(filePath){
1329:   if(process.platform!=='win32')return {ok:false,reason:'Windows OCR은 Windows에서만 사용할 수 있습니다.'};
1330:   const script=String.raw`$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'
1331: [Console]::OutputEncoding=[System.Text.Encoding]::UTF8
1332: Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Runtime.WindowsRuntime
1333: $null=[Windows.Storage.StorageFile,Windows.Storage,ContentType=WindowsRuntime]
1353: [PSCustomObject]@{text=$r.Text;language=$e.RecognizerLanguage.LanguageTag}|ConvertTo-Json -Compress`;
1354:   const r=await runProcess('powershell.exe',['-NoProfile','-NonInteractive','-ExecutionPolicy','Bypass','-Command',script],{env:{...process.env,UEP_OCR_PATH:filePath}});
1355:   if(!r.ok)return {ok:false,reason:(r.stderr||r.stdout||'Windows OCR 실행 실패').trim().slice(0,1200)};
1356:   try{const data=JSON.parse(r.stdout.trim());return {ok:true,engine:'Windows OCR',text:String(data.text||'').trim(),detail:String(data.language||'사용자 언어')};}catch{return {ok:false,reason:'Windows OCR 결과를 해석하지 못했습니다.'};}
1357: }
1358: async function ocrInputCenterFile(fileValue=''){
1359:   const filePath=await resolveInputAsset(fileValue)||String(fileValue||'');if(!filePath)return {ok:false,status:'OCR오류',reason:'OCR 원본 파일을 찾지 못했습니다.'};
1360:   const ext=path.extname(filePath).toLowerCase();if(!['.jpg','.jpeg','.png','.bmp','.tif','.tiff'].includes(ext))return {ok:false,status:'지원대기',reason:'현재 자동 OCR은 JPG·PNG 등 이미지 파일을 우선 지원합니다.'};
1361:   let r=await ocrWithWindowsMedia(filePath);if(!r.ok)r=await ocrWithTesseract(filePath);
1362:   if(!r.ok)return {ok:false,status:'OCR오류',reason:r.reason||'로컬 OCR에 실패했습니다.'};
1363:   const text=String(r.text||'').replace(/\r/g,'').trim();return {ok:true,status:text?'OCR완료':'OCR확인필요',engine:r.engine,detail:r.detail,text,chars:text.length};
1364: }
1365: async function ocrInputCenterBatch(payload={}){
1366:   const files=Array.isArray(payload.files)?payload.files:[];const results=await mapWithConcurrency(files,2,async f=>({key:String(f.key||''),path:String(f.path||''),...(await ocrInputCenterFile(String(f.path||'')))}));return {ok:true,count:results.length,results};
1367: }

1369: async function appendSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, range, values) {
1370:   const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(range)}:append`);
1371:   url.searchParams.set("valueInputOption", "USER_ENTERED");
1372:   url.searchParams.set("insertDataOption", "INSERT_ROWS");
1373:   const response = await fetch(url, {
1374:     method: "POST",
1375:     headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
1376:     body: JSON.stringify({ majorDimension: "ROWS", values }),
1377:   });
1378:   if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`공지 저장 실패 (${response.status})`);
1379:   return response.json();
1380: }
1381: 
1382: async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limit, worker) {
1383:   const queue = Array.from(items || []);
1384:   const results = new Array(queue.length);
1385:   let cursor = 0;
1386:   const runners = Array.from({ length: Math.max(1, Math.min(limit, queue.length || 1)) }, async () => {
1387:     while (true) {
1388:       const index = cursor++;
1389:       if (index >= queue.length) return;
1390:       results[index] = await worker(queue[index], index);
1391:     }
1392:   });
1393:   await Promise.all(runners);
1394:   return results;
1395: }

1398: function inputCenterReportPairs(meta={}){
1399:   const fields=Array.isArray(meta.fields)?meta.fields:[];
1400:   const skip=/^(연락처|전화번호|휴대폰|서명|이메일|학번|성명|이름|학년|반|수정시간|제출시간|제출일시)$/;
1401:   return fields.map((f,index)=>({label:String(f?.label||`문항 ${index+1}`).trim(),value:String(f?.value||'').trim()})).filter(x=>x.value&&!skip.test(x.label));
1402: }

1407: function normalizeSelectedReportProgramName(value=""){
1408:   return String(value||"").normalize("NFKC").trim().replace(/\s*\((?:\d{4}[./-]\d{1,2}[./-]\d{1,2}\.?)?(?:\s*[~～\-–—]\s*\d{4}[./-]\d{1,2}[./-]\d{1,2}\.?)?\)\s*$/g,"").replace(/\s+/g," ").trim();
1409: }
1410: function reportDateTimeText(value=""){
1411:   if(value instanceof Date&&!Number.isNaN(value.getTime()))return `${value.getFullYear()}-${String(value.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(value.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')} ${String(value.getHours()).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(value.getMinutes()).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(value.getSeconds()).padStart(2,'0')}`;
1412:   const raw=String(value||"").trim();
1413:   const kr=raw.match(/^(\d{4})[.\/-]\s*(\d{1,2})[.\/-]\s*(\d{1,2})\.?\s*(오전|오후)?\s*(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::(\d{2}))?/);
1414:   if(kr){let hour=Number(kr[5]);if(kr[4]==='오후'&&hour<12)hour+=12;if(kr[4]==='오전'&&hour===12)hour=0;return `${kr[1]}-${String(kr[2]).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(kr[3]).padStart(2,'0')} ${String(hour).padStart(2,'0')}:${kr[6]}:${kr[7]||'00'}`;}
1415:   const m=raw.match(/^(\d{4})[./-](\d{1,2})[./-](\d{1,2})(?:\s+|T)(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::(\d{2}))?/);
1416:   return m?`${m[1]}-${String(m[2]).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(m[3]).padStart(2,'0')} ${String(m[4]).padStart(2,'0')}:${m[5]}:${m[6]||'00'}`:raw;
1417: }

1424: async function repairSelectedReportTailGaps08204(token,current=[]){

1833: function ymd(value = new Date()) {
1834:   const date = value instanceof Date ? value : new Date(value);
1835:   return `${date.getFullYear()}${String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}${String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
1836: }
1837: 
1838: function mondayOf(dateValue) {
1839:   const date = dateValue instanceof Date ? new Date(dateValue) : new Date(dateValue || Date.now());
1840:   date.setHours(12, 0, 0, 0);
1841:   const day = date.getDay();
1842:   date.setDate(date.getDate() - (day === 0 ? 6 : day - 1));
1843:   return date;
1844: }
1845: 
1846: async function fetchJson(url, timeoutMs = 12000) {
1847:   const controller = new AbortController();
1848:   const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);
1849:   try {
1850:     const response = await fetch(url, {
1851:       signal: controller.signal,
1852:       headers: { "user-agent": "UEP/0.42.7 School Operations" },
1853:     });
1854:     if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`공공데이터 연결 실패 (${response.status})`);
1855:     return await response.json();
1856:   } finally {
1857:     clearTimeout(timer);
1858:   }
1859: }
1860: 
1861: function neisRows(payload, key) {
1862:   const group = payload?.[key];
1863:   if (!Array.isArray(group)) return [];
1864:   const rowBlock = group.find((item) => Array.isArray(item?.row));
1865:   return rowBlock?.row || [];
1866: }
1867: 
1868: function neisResultMessage(payload, key) {
1869:   const result = payload?.RESULT || payload?.[key]?.[0]?.head?.find?.((item) => item.RESULT)?.RESULT;
1870:   if (!result) return "";
1871:   return result.MESSAGE || result.CODE || "";
1872: }
1873: 
1874: function neisUrl(dataset, params, apiKey = "") {
1875:   const url = new URL(`https://open.neis.go.kr/hub/${dataset}`);
1876:   url.searchParams.set("Type", "json");
1877:   url.searchParams.set("pIndex", "1");
1878:   url.searchParams.set("pSize", "100");
1879:   if (apiKey) url.searchParams.set("KEY", apiKey);
1880:   Object.entries(params || {}).forEach(([key, value]) => {
1881:     if (value !== "" && value !== null && value !== undefined) url.searchParams.set(key, String(value));
1882:   });
1883:   return url.toString();
1884: }

1886: async function findNeisSchool({ schoolName, officeCode = "", schoolCode = "", apiKey = "" }) {
1887:   if (officeCode && schoolCode) return { schoolName, officeCode, schoolCode };
1888:   const payload = await fetchJson(neisUrl("schoolInfo", { SCHUL_NM: schoolName }, apiKey));
1889:   const rows = neisRows(payload, "schoolInfo");
1890:   const exact = rows.find((row) => String(row.SCHUL_NM || "").replace(/\s/g, "") === String(schoolName || "").replace(/\s/g, ""));
1891:   const row = exact || rows[0];
1892:   if (!row) throw new Error(neisResultMessage(payload, "schoolInfo") || `${schoolName} 학교정보를 찾지 못했습니다.`);
1893:   return {
1894:     schoolName: row.SCHUL_NM || schoolName,
1895:     officeCode: row.ATPT_OFCDC_SC_CODE,
1896:     schoolCode: row.SD_SCHUL_CODE,
1897:     officeName: row.ATPT_OFCDC_SC_NM || "",
1898:     address: row.ORG_RDNMA || row.ORG_RDNZC || "",
1899:   };
1900: }

1902: function cleanMealText(value) {
1903:   return String(value || "")
1904:     .replace(/<br\s*\/?\s*>/gi, "\n")
1905:     .replace(/\([^)]*\)/g, (match) => match)
1906:     .split(/\n+/)
1907:     .map((item) => item.trim())
1908:     .filter(Boolean);
1909: }

1911: async function fetchNeisMeals(school, targetDate, apiKey = "") {
1912:   const payload = await fetchJson(neisUrl("mealServiceDietInfo", {
1913:     ATPT_OFCDC_SC_CODE: school.officeCode,
1914:     SD_SCHUL_CODE: school.schoolCode,
1915:     MLSV_YMD: ymd(targetDate),
1916:   }, apiKey));
1917:   const rows = neisRows(payload, "mealServiceDietInfo");
1918:   return rows.map((row) => ({
1919:     date: row.MLSV_YMD || ymd(targetDate),
1920:     mealType: row.MMEAL_SC_NM || "급식",
1921:     menu: cleanMealText(row.DDISH_NM),
1922:     origin: cleanMealText(row.ORPLC_INFO),
1923:     calories: row.CAL_INFO || "",
1924:     nutrition: cleanMealText(row.NTR_INFO),
1925:   }));
1926: }

1928: function neisDateToLocalDate(value) {
1929:   const text = String(value || "").replace(/\D/g, "");
1930:   if (!/^\d{8}$/.test(text)) return null;
1931:   const date = new Date(Number(text.slice(0,4)), Number(text.slice(4,6))-1, Number(text.slice(6,8)), 12, 0, 0);
1932:   return Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? null : date;
1933: }
1934: 
1935: function expectedSchoolPeriods(value) {
1936:   const date = value instanceof Date ? value : neisDateToLocalDate(value);
1937:   const weekdayNo = date?.getDay();
1938:   if (weekdayNo === 3) return 5;
1939:   return [1,2,4,5].includes(weekdayNo) ? 7 : 0;
1940: }

1942: async function fetchNeisTimetable(school, targetDate, grade, classNo, apiKey = "") {
1943:   const monday = mondayOf(targetDate);
1944:   const dates = Array.from({ length: 5 }, (_, index) => {
1963:       GRADE: grade,
1964:       pIndex: 1,
1965:       pSize: 1000,
1966:     };
1967:     // NEIS 시간표 API는 환경에 따라 단일일자 필터명이 다르게 적용되는 사례가 있다.
1968:     // ALL_TI_YMD 방식과 공식 기간(TI_FROM_YMD/TI_TO_YMD) 방식을 동시에 조회하고 합쳐
1969:     // 기본 5건만 반환되는 잘못된 필터 적용을 방지한다.
1970:     const dateParamSets = [
1971:       { mode: "ALL_TI_YMD", params: { ALL_TI_YMD: dateKey } },
1972:       { mode: "TI_RANGE", params: { TI_FROM_YMD: dateKey, TI_TO_YMD: dateKey } },
1973:     ];
1974:     const fetchVariants = async (candidate) => {
1975:       const variants = await Promise.all(dateParamSets.map(async ({ mode, params }) => {
1976:         const url = neisUrl("hisTimetable", { ...common, ...params, CLASS_NM: candidate }, apiKey);
1977:         try { return { mode, candidate, url, payload: await fetchJson(url) }; }
1978:         catch (error) { return { mode, candidate, url, payload: { __error: error.message } }; }
1979:       }));
1980:       return variants;
1981:     };
1982:     const [exactVariants, ...sweepVariantGroups] = await Promise.all([
1983:       fetchVariants(classNo),
1984:       ...classCandidates.map((candidate) => fetchVariants(candidate)),
1985:     ]);
1986:     const exactUrl = exactVariants.find((entry) => entry.mode === "TI_RANGE")?.url || exactVariants[0]?.url || "";
1987:     return { exactVariants, sweepVariantGroups, exactUrl };
1988:   };
1989:   const payloads = await Promise.all(dates.map(requestDay));
1990:   const toLesson = (row, source) => ({
1991:     period: Number(row.PERIO || 0),
1992:     subject: String(row.ITRT_CNTNT || row.ITRT_CNTNT_NM || "-").trim() || "-",
1993:     grade: row.GRADE || String(grade),
1994:     classNo: row.CLASS_NM || String(classNo),
1995:     date: row.ALL_TI_YMD || "",
1996:     source,
1997:   });
1998:   const days = dates.map((date, index) => {
1999:     const pair = payloads[index] || {};
2000:     const exactVariants = Array.isArray(pair.exactVariants) ? pair.exactVariants : [];
2001:     const sweepVariantGroups = Array.isArray(pair.sweepVariantGroups) ? pair.sweepVariantGroups : [];
2002:     const exactRows = exactVariants.flatMap((entry) => entry?.payload?.__error ? [] : neisRows(entry.payload, "hisTimetable"));
2003:     const sweepVariants = sweepVariantGroups.flat();
2004:     const sweepRows = sweepVariants.flatMap((entry) => entry?.payload?.__error ? [] : neisRows(entry.payload, "hisTimetable"));
2005:     const dedupedRows = [];
2006:     const seen = new Set();
2007:     for (const row of sweepRows) {
2008:       const key = [row.ALL_TI_YMD,row.GRADE,row.CLASS_NM,row.PERIO,row.ITRT_CNTNT].map(v=>String(v??"")).join("|");
2009:       if (!seen.has(key)) { seen.add(key); dedupedRows.push(row); }
2010:     }
2011:     const classValues = [...new Set(dedupedRows.map((row) => String(row.CLASS_NM ?? "").trim()).filter(Boolean))]
2012:       .sort((a,b)=>Number(a)-Number(b));
2013:     const matchingRows = dedupedRows.filter((row) => {
2014:       const rowGrade = String(row.GRADE ?? "").trim();
2015:       return (!rowGrade || rowGrade === String(grade)) && normalizeClass(row.CLASS_NM) === wantedClass;
2016:     });
2017:     const exactLessons = exactRows.map((row) => toLesson(row, "학급 직접조회"));
2018:     const fallbackLessons = matchingRows.map((row) => toLesson(row, "1~9반 교차조회"));
2019:     const mergedByPeriod = new Map();
2020:     [...fallbackLessons, ...exactLessons]
2021:       .filter((item) => item.period > 0)
2022:       .sort((a, b) => a.period - b.period)
2023:       .forEach((item) => {
2024:         const current = mergedByPeriod.get(item.period);
2025:         if (!current || current.subject === "-" || item.source === "학급 직접조회") mergedByPeriod.set(item.period, item);
2026:       });
2027:     const rawLessons = [...mergedByPeriod.values()].sort((a, b) => a.period - b.period);
2028:     const directPeriods = new Set(exactLessons.map((item) => item.period).filter(Boolean));
2029:     const recoveredPeriods = rawLessons.filter((item) => item.source === "1~9반 교차조회" && !directPeriods.has(item.period)).map((item) => item.period);
2030:     return {
2031:       date: ymd(date),
2032:       weekday: ["일", "월", "화", "수", "목", "금", "토"][date.getDay()],
2033:       error: exactVariants.map((entry) => entry?.payload?.__error).filter(Boolean).join(" / "),
2034:       rawRowCount: exactRows.length,
2035:       gradeWideRowCount: dedupedRows.length,
2036:       gradeMatchedRowCount: matchingRows.length,
2037:       gradeClassValues: classValues,
2038:       recoveredPeriods,
2039:       rawPeriods: rawLessons.map((item) => item.period),
2040:       rawLessons,
2041:       rawResponse: {
2042:         exact: exactVariants.map(x=>({mode:x.mode,url:String(x.url||"").replace(/([?&]KEY=)[^&]+/i,"$1***"),payload:x.payload})),
2043:         classSweep: sweepVariants.map(x=>({mode:x.mode,classNo:x.candidate,url:String(x.url||"").replace(/([?&]KEY=)[^&]+/i,"$1***"),payload:x.payload}))
2044:       },
2045:       exactUrl: String(pair.exactUrl || "").replace(/([?&]KEY=)[^&]+/i,"$1***"),
2046:       gradeUrl: "1~9반 개별 정식 조회",
2047:       lessons: rawLessons,
2048:     };
2049:   });
2050:   const diagnostics = days.map((day) => {
2051:     const expected = expectedSchoolPeriods(day.date);
2052:     const periods = day.lessons.map((lesson) => Number(lesson.period || 0)).filter(Boolean);
2053:     const maxPeriod = periods.length ? Math.max(...periods) : 0;
2054:     return {
2055:       date: day.date,
2056:       weekday: day.weekday,
2057:       rowCount: day.lessons.length,
2058:       maxPeriod,
2059:       expected,
2060:       complete: !day.lessons.length || maxPeriod >= expected,
2061:       missingPeriods: expected ? Array.from({length: expected}, (_,i)=>i+1).filter(period=>!periods.includes(period)) : [],
2062:       recoveredPeriods: day.recoveredPeriods || [],
2063:       exactRowCount: day.rawRowCount || 0,
2064:       gradeWideRowCount: day.gradeWideRowCount || 0,
2065:       gradeMatchedRowCount: day.gradeMatchedRowCount || 0,
2066:       error: day.error || "",
2067:     };
2068:   });
2069:   return { year, semester, monday: ymd(monday), days, diagnostics, queryMode: "ALL_TI_YMD + TI_FROM_YMD/TI_TO_YMD 이중조회 · 1~9반 교차검증" };
2070: }

2082: async function fetchNeisDashboard(options = {}) {
2083:   const targetDate = options.date ? new Date(`${options.date}T12:00:00`) : new Date();
2084:   try {
2085:     let apiKey = "";
2086:     if (options.useApiKey) {
2087:       const saved = await readEncrypted(neisApiKeyPath());
2088:       apiKey = String(saved?.key || "").trim();
2089:       if (!apiKey) throw new Error("NEIS 인증키가 등록되지 않았습니다.");
2090:     }
2091:     const school = await findNeisSchool({
2092:       schoolName: options.schoolName || "운호고등학교",
2093:       officeCode: options.officeCode || "",
2094:       schoolCode: options.schoolCode || "",
2095:       apiKey,
2096:     });
2097:     const mealsPromise = fetchNeisMeals(school, targetDate, apiKey);
2098:     let timetableSource = "NEIS";
2099:     let timetable;
2100:     try {
2101:       const comcigan = await fetchComciganTimetable({ ...options, date: targetDate, comciganSchoolCode: options.comciganSchoolCode || "69092" });
2102:       timetable = comcigan.timetable;
2103:       timetableSource = comcigan.offline ? "컴시간 저장자료" : "컴시간 웹";
2104:     } catch (comciganError) {
2105:       timetable = await fetchNeisTimetable(school, targetDate, options.grade || "1", options.classNo || "1", apiKey);
2106:       timetableSource = "NEIS 대체";
2107:       timetable.comciganError = comciganError?.message || String(comciganError);
2108:     }
2109:     const meals = await mealsPromise;
2132:     throw error;
2133:   }
2134: }

2136: async function readQuickLaunchSettings08197() {
2137:   try {
2138:     const value = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(quickLaunchPath(), "utf8"));
2139:     return { messengerPath: String(value?.messengerPath || ""), comtimePath: String(value?.comtimePath || "") };
2140:   } catch { return { messengerPath: "", comtimePath: "" }; }
2141: }
2142: async function writeQuickLaunchSettings08197(value) {
2143:   const links = value?.settings?.links || {};
2144:   const payload = { messengerPath: String(links.messengerPath || ""), comtimePath: String(links.comtimePath || ""), savedAt: new Date().toISOString() };
2145:   if (!payload.messengerPath && !payload.comtimePath) return;
2146:   await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(quickLaunchPath()), { recursive: true });
2147:   await fs.writeFile(quickLaunchPath(), JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2), "utf8");
2148: }
2149: async function readState() {
2150:   let value = null;
2151:   try { value = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(statePath(), "utf8")); } catch {}
2152:   const quick = await readQuickLaunchSettings08197();
2153:   if (!value && (quick.messengerPath || quick.comtimePath)) value = {};
2154:   if (value && (quick.messengerPath || quick.comtimePath)) {
2155:     value.settings = value.settings && typeof value.settings === "object" ? value.settings : {};
2156:     value.settings.links = value.settings.links && typeof value.settings.links === "object" ? value.settings.links : {};
2157:     if (quick.messengerPath) value.settings.links.messengerPath = quick.messengerPath;
2158:     if (quick.comtimePath) value.settings.links.comtimePath = quick.comtimePath;
2159:   }
2160:   return value;
2161: }
2162: let stateWriteChain = Promise.resolve();
2163: async function writeStateNow(value) {
2164:   const target = statePath();
2165:   const dir = path.dirname(target);
2166:   await fs.mkdir(dir, { recursive: true });
2167: 
2168:   // Each save gets its own temporary file. A fixed ".tmp" name can be
2169:   // removed by a concurrent save, producing ENOENT during copy/rename.
2170:   const temp = path.join(dir, `school-board-state.${process.pid}.${Date.now()}.${crypto.randomBytes(4).toString("hex")}.tmp`);
2171:   const payload = JSON.stringify(value, null, 2);
2172:   try {
2173:     await fs.writeFile(temp, payload, "utf8");
2174:     try {
2175:       await fs.rename(temp, target);
2176:     } catch (renameError) {
2177:       // Windows may reject replacement by rename when the destination exists.
2178:       // Fall back to a direct write; this keeps user data available instead
2179:       // of failing the entire state:save IPC call.
2180:       await fs.writeFile(target, payload, "utf8");
2181:       try { await fs.unlink(temp); } catch {}
2182:     }
2183:   } finally {
2184:     try { await fs.unlink(temp); } catch {}
2185:   }
2186:   try { await writeQuickLaunchSettings08197(value); } catch (error) { console.warn("[UEP] quick launch backup failed", error?.message || error); }
2187:   return { ok: true, savedAt: new Date().toISOString() };
2188: }
2189: function writeState(value) {
2190:   // Serialize renderer saves so rapid UI updates cannot race each other.
2191:   const task = stateWriteChain.then(() => writeStateNow(value));
2192:   stateWriteChain = task.catch(() => {});
2193:   return task;
2194: }

2667:   return {ok:true,local:true,mode:"login_identity",account:"",userOAuth:false,autoConnected:true,loginIdentityGateway:true,approvalRequired:false,tokenExchangeRequired:false,reason:"UEP 로그인 이름·이메일로 교사·담임을 식별합니다."};
2668: });

2734: function inputCenterNatureToStoredType(nature=""){
2735:   const n=String(nature||"");
2736:   if(n==="보고서")return "보고서·탐구보고서";
2737:   if(n==="신청")return "프로그램 신청서";
2738:   if(n==="만족도조사")return "만족도·설문조사";
2739:   if(n==="출결·명단")return "출결·참여명단";
2740:   return n||"기타";
2741: }
2742: function inputCenterProgramIdRaw(id=""){return String(id||"").replace(/^(common|selected)-/,'');}
2743: function inputCenterSemester(value="",submittedAt=""){
2744:   const t=String(value||""); if(/(^|[^0-9])1([^0-9]|$)|1학기/.test(t))return "1학기"; if(/(^|[^0-9])2([^0-9]|$)|2학기/.test(t))return "2학기";
2745:   const m=String(submittedAt||"").match(/\d{4}[-/.](\d{1,2})/);return m&&Number(m[1])<=7?"1학기":"2학기";
2746: }
2747: async function classifyInputCenterBatch(payload={}){
2748:   try{
2749:     const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());if(!validateServiceAccount(account))throw new Error('Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 필요합니다.');
2750:     const batchId=String(payload.batchId||'').trim();if(!batchId)throw new Error('입력 배치를 선택해 주세요.');
2751:     const token=await getSheetsToken(account);const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_INPUT_CENTER_SPREADSHEET_ID,["'02_자료원본'!A3:X10000"]);const rows=(result?.[0]?.values||[]).slice(1);
2752:     let count=0;const storedType=inputCenterNatureToStoredType(payload.nature);const pid=String(payload.programId||'');const pk=String(payload.programKind||'');const note=pid?`ROUTE:PROGRAM_REPORT:${pid}`:'';const writes=[];
2753:     for(let i=0;i<rows.length;i++)if(String(rows[i]?.[1]||'')===batchId){const sheetRow=i+4;const current=Array.from({length:24},(_,j)=>rows[i]?.[j]??'');current[3]=storedType;current[4]=payload.nature==='보고서'?'보고서 제출':payload.nature;let meta={};try{meta=JSON.parse(String(current[16]||''));}catch{}current[6]=inputCenterSemester(payload.programSemester||'',meta.submittedAt||'');current[17]='검수완료';current[19]=pid;current[20]=pk;current[23]=note||current[23]||'';writes.push({range:`'02_자료원본'!A${sheetRow}:X${sheetRow}`,values:[current]});count++;}
2754:     await batchUpdateSheetValues(token,UEP_INPUT_CENTER_SPREADSHEET_ID,writes);
2755:     const sourcePatch=await fetchInputCenterSourceData();return {ok:true,count,batchId,externalInputs:sourcePatch.externalInputs,inputCenterSyncedAt:sourcePatch.inputCenterSyncedAt};
2756:   }catch(error){return {ok:false,reason:error?.message||String(error)};}
2757: }
2758: async function normalizeInputCenterBatch(payload={}){
2759:   try{
2760:     const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());if(!validateServiceAccount(account))throw new Error('Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 필요합니다.');
2761:     const records=Array.isArray(payload.records)?payload.records:[];if(!records.length)throw new Error('정규화할 자료가 없습니다.');
2762:     if(String(payload.nature||'')!=='보고서')throw new Error('현재 정규화 자동적용은 보고서부터 지원합니다. 신청·만족도·명단은 다음 라우팅 단계에서 연결합니다.');
2763:     const kind=String(payload.programKind||'')==='selected'?'selected':'common';
2764:     const targetSheet=kind==='selected'?'21_선택보고서_정규화':'20_공통보고서_정규화';
2765:     const token=await getSheetsToken(account);
2766:     // REPORT_TEXT_V2 표준 스키마 A:X. 원본유형/원본ID로 출처를 구분하므로 입력센터 자료도 기존 데이터 바로 다음 행에 연속 저장합니다.
2767:     const DATA_START_ROW=4, INPUT_END_ROW=5000;
2768:     const existingResult=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_PROCESSING_SPREADSHEET_ID,[`'${targetSheet}'!A3:X${INPUT_END_ROW}`]);
2769:     const matrix=existingResult?.[0]?.values||[];
2770:     const existing=matrix.slice(1).map((row,index)=>({row, sheetRow:index+4})).filter(x=>(x.row||[]).some(v=>String(v??'').trim()));
2771:     const prefix=kind==='selected'?'ISR':'ICR';
2772:     let maxInputId=0;for(const item of existing){const id=String(item.row?.[0]||'');if(!id.startsWith(prefix+'-'))continue;const n=Number(id.match(/(\d+)$/)?.[1]||0);if(n>maxInputId)maxInputId=n;}
2773:     const programId=inputCenterProgramIdRaw(payload.programId||records[0]?.programId||'');
2774:     const programName=String(payload.programName||'');
2775:     if(!programId)throw new Error('보고서는 연결 프로그램ID가 필요합니다. 02 검수·분류에서 프로그램을 확정해 주세요.');
2776:     const hashText=value=>crypto.createHash('sha256').update(String(value||''),'utf8').digest('hex');
2777:     const canonicalExistingHash=row=>{
2778:       const declared=String(row?.[23]||'').trim();if(declared)return declared;
2779:       const normalized=String(row?.[12]||'').trim();
2780:       return hashText([row?.[2]||row?.[3]||'',row?.[7]||'',normalized].map(v=>String(v||'').trim()).join('\n'));
2781:     };
2782:     const prepared=[];let next=maxInputId+1;
2783:     for(const rec of records){
2784:       let meta={};try{meta=JSON.parse(String(rec.normalizedText||''));}catch{}
2785:       const submittedAt=String(meta.submittedAt||rec.createdAt||'');
2786:       const pairs=inputCenterReportPairs(meta);
2787:       const reviewedContent=String(rec.reviewText||'').trim();
2788:       const normalizedContent=reviewedContent||inputCenterReportText(meta)||String(rec.rawText||'').trim();
2789:       if(!normalizedContent)continue;
2790:       const signature=(Array.isArray(meta.fields)?meta.fields:[]).find(f=>/서명|동의/.test(String(f.label||'')))?.value||'';
2791:       const semester=inputCenterSemester(payload.programSemester||'',submittedAt);
2792:       const studentKey=String(rec.studentId||rec.studentNo||'').trim();
2793:       const logicalKey=`${rec.schoolYear||'2026'}|${semester}|${studentKey}|${programId}`;
2794:       const sourceType='INPUT_CENTER';
2795:       const sourceId=String(rec.id||'').trim()||`INPUT-${hashText(`${rec.batchId||''}|${studentKey}|${submittedAt}`).slice(0,20)}`;
2796:       const sourceBatchId=String(rec.batchId||payload.batchId||'').trim();
2797:       const sourceModifiedAt=String(rec.createdAt||submittedAt||new Date().toISOString());
2798:       const contentHash=hashText([studentKey,programId,normalizedContent].map(v=>String(v||'').trim()).join('\n'));
2799:       const row=[
2800:         `${prefix}-${String(next++).padStart(5,'0')}`,kind==='selected'?'선택활동':'공통활동',rec.studentId||'',rec.studentNo||'',rec.name||'',rec.schoolYear||'2026',semester,
2801:         programId,programName||meta.programName||'',submittedAt,'Y',/^(O|Y|동의|예|동의함)$/i.test(String(signature).trim())?'동의함':'',
2802:         normalizedContent,JSON.stringify(pairs),'REPORT_TEXT_V2',rec.id||'',rec.studentId?'정상':'확인필요',sourceType,sourceId,sourceBatchId,logicalKey,sourceModifiedAt,'정상',contentHash
2803:       ];
2804:       prepared.push({rec,row,sourceType,sourceId,logicalKey,contentHash,submittedAt});
2805:     }
2806:     if(!prepared.length)throw new Error('정규화할 보고서 본문을 찾지 못했습니다. 02 검수내용을 확인해 주세요.');
2807:     let newCount=0,duplicateCount=0,updateCount=0;const actions=[];
2808:     for(const p of prepared){
2809:       const sameSource=existing.find(item=>String(item.row?.[17]||'')===p.sourceType&&String(item.row?.[18]||'')===p.sourceId);
2810:       if(sameSource){
2811:         const old=sameSource.row;
2812:         if(String(old?.[22]||'정상')!=='삭제됨'&&canonicalExistingHash(old)===p.contentHash){duplicateCount++;actions.push({type:'duplicate',row:p.row,rec:p.rec});continue;}
2813:         p.row[0]=old?.[0]||p.row[0];updateCount++;actions.push({type:'update',row:p.row,rec:p.rec,sheetRow:sameSource.sheetRow});continue;
2814:       }
2815:       const sameContent=existing.find(item=>{
2816:         const r=item.row;if(String(r?.[22]||'정상')==='삭제됨')return false;
2817:         return String(r?.[20]||'')===p.logicalKey&&canonicalExistingHash(r)===p.contentHash;
2818:       });
2819:       if(sameContent){duplicateCount++;actions.push({type:'duplicate',row:p.row,rec:p.rec});continue;}
2820:       newCount++;actions.push({type:'new',row:p.row,rec:p.rec});
2821:     }
2822:     if(!payload.confirmed)return {ok:true,requiresConfirmation:true,targetSheet,programName,newCount,duplicateCount,updateCount,errorCount:0};
2823:     // 기존 입력센터 제출 갱신도 한 번의 batchUpdate로 처리하여 Write quota를 소모하지 않습니다.
2824:     const reportUpdates=actions.filter(a=>a.type==='update').map(a=>({range:`'${targetSheet}'!A${a.sheetRow}:X${a.sheetRow}`,values:[a.row]}));
2825:     await batchUpdateSheetValues(token,UEP_PROCESSING_SPREADSHEET_ID,reportUpdates);
2826:     // 신규 입력센터 정규화는 출처별 물리 구역을 나누지 않고 실제 마지막 데이터 행 바로 다음에 연속 저장합니다.
2827:     const lastUsed=existing.length?Math.max(...existing.map(item=>item.sheetRow)):DATA_START_ROW-1;
2828:     const news=actions.filter(a=>a.type==='new');
2829:     if(news.length){
2830:       const start=Math.max(DATA_START_ROW,lastUsed+1),end=start+news.length-1;
2831:       if(end>INPUT_END_ROW)throw new Error(`보고서 정규화 시트의 저장 가능 행을 초과했습니다. (${DATA_START_ROW}~${INPUT_END_ROW}행)`);
2832:       await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_PROCESSING_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'${targetSheet}'!A${start}:X${end}`,news.map(a=>a.row));
2833:     }
2834:     // 입력센터 원본에서 삭제된 자료는 정규화 원장에서 상태만 '삭제됨'으로 남겨 이력을 보존합니다.
2835:     const srcResult=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_INPUT_CENTER_SPREADSHEET_ID,["'02_자료원본'!A3:X10000"]);
2836:     const src=(srcResult?.[0]?.values||[]).slice(1);const liveSourceIds=new Set(src.map(r=>String(r?.[0]||'')).filter(Boolean));
2837:     const deleteWrites=[];
2838:     for(const item of existing){
2839:       const row=item.row;if(String(row?.[17]||'')!=='INPUT_CENTER')continue;
2840:       const sid=String(row?.[18]||'');if(!sid||liveSourceIds.has(sid)||String(row?.[22]||'')==='삭제됨')continue;
2841:       deleteWrites.push({range:`'${targetSheet}'!W${item.sheetRow}:W${item.sheetRow}`,values:[['삭제됨']]});
2842:     }
2843:     await batchUpdateSheetValues(token,UEP_PROCESSING_SPREADSHEET_ID,deleteWrites);
2844: 
2845:     // v0.80.45: 쓰기 응답만 믿지 않고 목적지 원장을 다시 읽어 실제 저장을 검증합니다.
2846:     // 같은 원본ID가 있거나, 동일 논리키+내용해시가 실제로 존재해야만 해당 원본을 완료 처리합니다.
2847:     const verifyResult=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_PROCESSING_SPREADSHEET_ID,[`'${targetSheet}'!A3:X${INPUT_END_ROW}`]);
2848:     const verifyRows=(verifyResult?.[0]?.values||[]).slice(1).filter(row=>(row||[]).some(v=>String(v??'').trim()));
2849:     const actionVerified=a=>verifyRows.some(r=>{
2850:       if(String(r?.[22]||'정상')==='삭제됨')return false;
2851:       const sameSource=String(r?.[17]||'')===String(a.row?.[17]||'')&&String(r?.[18]||'')===String(a.row?.[18]||'');
2852:       const sameLogicalContent=String(r?.[20]||'')===String(a.row?.[20]||'')&&canonicalExistingHash(r)===String(a.row?.[23]||'');
2853:       return sameSource||sameLogicalContent;
2854:     });
2855:     const unverified=actions.filter(a=>!actionVerified(a));
2856:     if(unverified.length){
2857:       const sample=unverified.slice(0,5).map(a=>String(a.row?.[18]||a.rec?.id||'원본ID없음')).join(', ');
2858:       throw new Error(`정규화 목적지 저장 검증 실패 ${unverified.length}건${sample?` (${sample}${unverified.length>5?', …':''})`:''}. 입력센터 원본은 완료 처리하지 않았습니다.`);
2859:     }
2860: 
2861:     // 실제 목적지에 존재하는 준비건만 원본 처리상태를 완료합니다.
2862:     // 본문이 없어 prepared에서 제외된 원본까지 완료되는 기존 문제도 함께 차단합니다.
2863:     const verifiedIds=new Set(actions.map(a=>String(a.rec?.id||'')).filter(Boolean));const statusWrites=[];
2864:     for(let i=0;i<src.length;i++)if(verifiedIds.has(String(src[i]?.[0]||'')))statusWrites.push({range:`'02_자료원본'!R${i+4}:R${i+4}`,values:[['정규화완료']]});
2865:     await batchUpdateSheetValues(token,UEP_INPUT_CENTER_SPREADSHEET_ID,statusWrites);
2866:     liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;return {ok:true,targetSheet,programName,newCount,duplicateCount,updateCount,errorCount:0,count:newCount+updateCount,verifiedCount:actions.length};
2867:   }catch(error){return {ok:false,reason:error?.message||String(error)};}
2868: }

2898: function noticePayloadRow(payload={}, id, nowIso, headerNames=[]) {
2899:   const now = new Date(nowIso);

3173:       // Node global fetch was unreliable on some school Windows networks ("fetch failed"),
3174:       // while Electron net.fetch uses Chromium's network stack and follows GitHub redirects.
3175:       let response;
3176:       try {
3177:         response=await net.fetch(downloadUrl,{
3178:           method:"GET",
3179:           redirect:"follow",
3180:           headers:{

3193:       catch(readError) { return {ok:false,reason:`업데이트 파일 수신 실패 · ${String(readError?.message||readError)}`}; }

3200:       const entries=await fs.readdir(stagePath,{withFileTypes:true});
3201:       let source=stagePath;
3202:       if(entries.length===1 && entries[0].isDirectory()) source=path.join(stagePath,entries[0].name);

3205:       const installDir=path.dirname(process.execPath);
3206:       // Fast folder-swap updater: the staged build is already fully extracted before UEP exits.
3207:       // Instead of mirroring the whole installation twice (backup + install), wait for UEP to
3208:       // release its files, rename the old folder, then move the staged folder into place. On the
3209:       // normal same-drive Windows setup these directory operations are effectively instantaneous.
3210:       const backupDir=path.join(path.dirname(installDir),`${path.basename(installDir)}.__uep_backup_${version.replace(/[^0-9A-Za-z._-]/g,'_')}`);
3211:       const scriptPath=path.join(updateRoot,"apply-update.ps1");
3212:       const q=v=>String(v).replace(/'/g,"''");
3213:       const logPath=path.join(updateRoot,"apply-update.log");
3214:       const newExePath=path.join(installDir,exeName);
3215:       const script=`$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'\n$pidToWait=${process.pid}\n$src='${q(source)}'\n$dst='${q(installDir)}'\n$backup='${q(backupDir)}'\n$exe='${q(newExePath)}'\n$log='${q(logPath)}'\nfunction Log($m){Add-Content -LiteralPath $log -Value ((Get-Date -Format o)+' '+$m)}\nLog 'updater-start'\nfor($i=0;$i -lt 80;$i++){if(-not (Get-Process -Id $pidToWait -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)){break};Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 100}\nif(Get-Process -Id $pidToWait -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue){Log 'parent-still-running'; taskkill /PID $pidToWait /T /F | Out-Null; Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 300}\nif(Test-Path -LiteralPath $backup){Remove-Item -LiteralPath $backup -Recurse -Force}\nLog 'swap-start'\ntry{\n  Rename-Item -LiteralPath $dst -NewName ([IO.Path]::GetFileName($backup))\n  Move-Item -LiteralPath $src -Destination $dst\n}catch{\n  Log ('swap-failed '+$_.Exception.Message)\n  if((Test-Path -LiteralPath $backup) -and -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $dst)){Rename-Item -LiteralPath $backup -NewName ([IO.Path]::GetFileName($dst))}\n  if(Test-Path -LiteralPath $exe){Start-Process -FilePath $exe -WorkingDirectory $dst}\n  exit 2\n}\nLog 'swap-done'\nStart-Process -FilePath $exe -WorkingDirectory $dst\nLog 'relaunch-requested'\nStart-Sleep -Seconds 2\ntry{if(Test-Path -LiteralPath $backup){Remove-Item -LiteralPath $backup -Recurse -Force;Log 'backup-cleaned'}}catch{Log ('backup-cleanup-deferred '+$_.Exception.Message)}\n`;
3224:       // This closes auxiliary BrowserWindows first, then quits Electron, with app.exit()
3225:       // only as a short fallback if Chromium child processes linger.
3226:       setTimeout(()=>{
3227:         try { destroyAuxiliaryWindows("update-apply"); } catch {}
3228:         try { BrowserWindow.getAllWindows().forEach(win=>{try{win.destroy();}catch{}}); } catch {}
3229:         try { beginCleanShutdown("update-apply"); } catch {}
3230:         try { app.quit(); } catch { try { app.exit(0); } catch {} }
3231:       },300);
3232:       return {ok:true,installing:true,version};
3233:     } catch(error){return {ok:false,reason:error?.message||"업데이트 적용 준비 중 오류가 발생했습니다."};}

3236:   ipcMain.handle("app:checkUpdate", async (_event, payload = {}) => {
3237:     try {
3238:       const rawUrl=String(payload.url||"").trim();
3239:       if (!/^https:\/\//i.test(rawUrl)) return { ok:false, reason:"HTTPS 업데이트 정보 주소가 필요합니다." };
3240: 
3241:       // Always resolve the freshest manifest we can find. During school-wide use,
3242:       // raw.githubusercontent.com / CDN caches can be stale or rate-limited, so:
3243:       //   1) derive GitHub Contents API + raw + jsDelivr candidates,
3244:       //   2) cache-bust non-API candidates,
3245:       //   3) read every reachable candidate and choose the HIGHEST semantic version.
3246:       const stamp=Date.now();
3247:       const candidates=[];
3248:       const push=(url,kind)=>{if(url && !candidates.some(x=>x.url===url))candidates.push({url,kind});};
3249:       let canonical=rawUrl;
3250:       try {
3251:         const u=new URL(rawUrl);
3252:         u.search=""; u.hash="";
3253:         canonical=u.toString();
3254:         if(u.hostname.toLowerCase()==="raw.githubusercontent.com"){
3255:           const parts=u.pathname.split('/').filter(Boolean);
3256:           if(parts.length>=4){
3257:             const [owner,repo,ref,...fileParts]=parts;
3258:             const filePath=fileParts.join('/');
3259:             push(`https://api.github.com/repos/${owner}/${repo}/contents/${filePath}?ref=${encodeURIComponent(ref)}`,'github-api');
3260:             push(`${u.toString()}?uep=${stamp}`,'github-raw');
3261:             push(`https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/${owner}/${repo}@${ref}/${filePath}?uep=${stamp}`,'jsdelivr');
3262:           }
3263:         } else {
3264:           push(`${u.toString()}${u.search?'&':'?'}uep=${stamp}`,'direct');
3265:         }
3292:           }else data=await response.json();
3293:           const version=String(data?.version||data?.latestVersion||'').trim();
3294:           if(!/^\d+\.\d+\.\d+/.test(version)){lastError=`${candidate.kind}: version 없음`;continue;}
3295:           successes.push({data,version,source:candidate.kind,url:candidate.url});
3296:         }catch(error){lastError=`${candidate.kind}: ${String(error?.message||error)}`;}
3297:       }
3298: 
3299:       if(!successes.length) return {ok:false,reason:`업데이트 정보 읽기 실패${lastStatus?` (${lastStatus})`:''}${lastError?` · ${lastError}`:''}`};
3300:       successes.sort((a,b)=>cmpVersion(b.version,a.version));
3301:       const chosen=successes[0];
3302:       const data=chosen.data;
3303:       const version=chosen.version;
3304:       const current=String(payload.currentVersion||"0.0.0");
3305:       const newer=cmpVersion(version,current)>0;
3306:       return {
3307:         ok:true,
3308:         version,
3309:         updateAvailable:newer,
3310:         downloadUrl:String(data.downloadUrl||data.url||""),
3311:         sha256:String(data.sha256||data.sha256sum||"").trim().toLowerCase(),
3312:         notes:String(data.notes||data.releaseNotes||""),
3313:         source:chosen.source,
3314:         checkedVersions:successes.map(x=>`${x.source}:${x.version}`)
3315:       };
3316:     } catch(error){return {ok:false,reason:error?.message||"업데이트 확인 중 오류가 발생했습니다."};}
3317:   });

3444: // __UEP_NEIS_RECORDBOOK_LOCAL_INSPECT_08102__
3445: // Read-only local inspection. No Google/Sheets/state write is called from this handler.
3446: ipcMain.handle("recordbook:inspectNeisExcel", async (event) => {
3447:   const owner=BrowserWindow.fromWebContents(event.sender);
3448:   const picked=await dialog.showOpenDialog(owner,{title:"나이스 교과 세특 엑셀 선택",properties:["openFile"],filters:[{name:"나이스 엑셀·CSV",extensions:["xlsx","xls","csv"]}]});
3449:   if(picked.canceled||!picked.filePaths[0])return {ok:false,canceled:true};
3450:   const filePath=picked.filePaths[0],ext=path.extname(filePath).toLowerCase();
3451:   try{
3452:     if(ext===".csv"){
3453:       const raw=await fs.readFile(filePath,"utf8");
3454:       const rows=[];let row=[],cell="",quoted=false;
3455:       for(let i=0;i<raw.length;i++){const ch=raw[i];if(ch==='"'&&quoted&&raw[i+1]==='"'){cell+='"';i++;continue;}if(ch==='"'){quoted=!quoted;continue;}if(ch===","&&!quoted){row.push(cell);cell="";continue;}if((ch==="\n"||ch==="\r")&&!quoted){if(ch==="\r"&&raw[i+1]==="\n")i++;row.push(cell);if(row.some(v=>String(v).trim()))rows.push(row);row=[];cell="";continue;}cell+=ch;}
3456:       row.push(cell);if(row.some(v=>String(v).trim()))rows.push(row);
3457:       return {ok:true,localOnly:true,fileName:path.basename(filePath),sheets:[{name:"CSV",rows}]};
3458:     }
3459:     const ps=String.raw`
3460: $ErrorActionPreference='Stop'
3461: [Console]::OutputEncoding=[Text.UTF8Encoding]::new($false);$OutputEncoding=[Console]::OutputEncoding
3462: $excel=$null;$books=$null;$book=$null;$result=@()
3463: try{
3464:   $excel=New-Object -ComObject Excel.Application;$excel.Visible=$false;$excel.DisplayAlerts=$false
3465:   $books=$excel.Workbooks;$book=$books.Open($env:UEP_NEIS_FILE,0,$true)
3466:   $sheetLimit=[Math]::Min(10,$book.Worksheets.Count)
3467:   for($s=1;$s -le $sheetLimit;$s++){
3468:     $ws=$book.Worksheets.Item($s);$used=$ws.UsedRange
3469:     $rowCount=[Math]::Min(5000,[int]$used.Rows.Count);$colCount=[Math]::Min(120,[int]$used.Columns.Count);$rows=@()
3470:     for($r=1;$r -le $rowCount;$r++){$line=@();for($col=1;$col -le $colCount;$col++){$line+=([string]$used.Cells.Item($r,$col).Text)};if(($line -join '').Trim().Length -gt 0){$rows+=,@($line)}}
3471:     $result+=@{name=[string]$ws.Name;rows=$rows}
3472:     [void][Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::ReleaseComObject($used);[void][Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::ReleaseComObject($ws)
3473:   }
3474:   $result|ConvertTo-Json -Depth 8 -Compress
3475: }finally{
3476:   if($book){$book.Close($false)};if($excel){$excel.Quit()}
3477:   if($book){[void][Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::ReleaseComObject($book)};if($books){[void][Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::ReleaseComObject($books)};if($excel){[void][Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::ReleaseComObject($excel)}
3478:   [GC]::Collect();[GC]::WaitForPendingFinalizers()
3479: }`;
3480:     const {execFile}=require("child_process");
3481:     const stdout=await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{execFile("powershell.exe",["-NoProfile","-NonInteractive","-ExecutionPolicy","Bypass","-Command",ps],{windowsHide:true,maxBuffer:64*1024*1024,env:{...process.env,UEP_NEIS_FILE:filePath}},(error,out,err)=>error?reject(new Error(String(err||error.message))):resolve(out));});
3482:     const sheets=JSON.parse(String(stdout||"[]").replace(/^\uFEFF/,""));
3483:     return {ok:true,localOnly:true,fileName:path.basename(filePath),sheets:Array.isArray(sheets)?sheets:[sheets]};
3484:   }catch(error){return {ok:false,localOnly:true,reason:"엑셀을 읽지 못했습니다. Microsoft Excel 설치 여부와 파일 형식을 확인해 주세요. "+String(error?.message||error)};}
3485: });

3488: // __UEP_SCHOOL_CONNECTION_RECOVERY_08103__
3489: // Recover only credentials already provisioned on this Windows user/PC.
3490: // Nothing is embedded in the app and no personal Google OAuth token is required.
3491: function schoolConnectionLegacyRoots(){
3492:   const appData=process.env.APPDATA||app.getPath("appData");
3493:   return [...new Set([
3494:     stableUserDataRoot(),legacyUserDataRoot(),
3495:     path.join(appData,"UNHO Education Platform"),
3496:     path.join(appData,"unho-education-platform"),
3497:     path.join(appData,"UEP"),
3498:     path.join(appData,"UEP School Board"),
3499:     path.join(appData,"School Board")
3500:   ].filter(Boolean).map(value=>path.resolve(value)))];
3501: }
3502: async function readCredentialRecoveryFrom(filePath){
3503:   const payload=JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(filePath,"utf8"));
3504:   if(!payload||payload.version!==1||payload.algorithm!=="aes-256-gcm")throw new Error("invalid recovery credential");
3505:   const salt=Buffer.from(payload.salt,"base64"),iv=Buffer.from(payload.iv,"base64"),tag=Buffer.from(payload.tag,"base64"),data=Buffer.from(payload.data,"base64");
3506:   const decipher=crypto.createDecipheriv("aes-256-gcm",recoveryKey(salt),iv);decipher.setAuthTag(tag);
3507:   return JSON.parse(Buffer.concat([decipher.update(data),decipher.final()]).toString("utf8"));
3508: }
3509: async function resolveSchoolServiceAccount(){
3510:   try{const current=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());if(validateServiceAccount(current))return current;}catch{}
3511:   for(const root of schoolConnectionLegacyRoots()){
3512:     for(const fileName of ["google-service-account.recovery.json","google-service-account.json"]){
3513:       const candidate=path.join(root,fileName);
3514:       try{
3515:         const recovered=fileName.endsWith("recovery.json")?await readCredentialRecoveryFrom(candidate):JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(candidate,"utf8"));
3516:         if(!validateServiceAccount(recovered))continue;
3517:         await saveEncrypted(credentialPath(),recovered);
3518:         console.log(`[UEP] 학교 공용 Google 연결을 기존 UEP 저장소에서 복구했습니다: ${path.basename(root)}`);
3519:         return recovered;
3520:       }catch{}
3521:     }
3522:   }
3523:   const error=new Error("이 PC에서 기존 학교 공용 Google 연결을 찾지 못했습니다. 관리자 배포본의 공용 연결 복구 상태를 확인해 주세요.");
3524:   error.code="UEP_SCHOOL_CONNECTION_NOT_PROVISIONED";throw error;
3525: }
